matched for a segment depending\\n557: // on the match type.\\n558: func (s *Server) matchConstraints(evalCtx map[string]string, constraints []storage.EvaluationConstraint, segmentMatchType core.MatchType, entityId string) (bool, string, error) {\\n559: \\tconstraintMatches := 0\\n560: \\n561: \\tvar reason string\\n562: \\n563: \\tfor _, c := range constraints {\\n564: \\t\\tv := reason, errs.ErrInvalid(\\\"unknown constraint type\\\")\\n584: \\t\\t}\\n585: \\n586: \\t\\tif err != nil {\\n587: \\t\\t\\ts.logger.Debug(\\\"error matching constraint\\\", zap.String(\\\"property\\\", c.Property), zap.Error(err))\\n588: \\t\\t\\t// don't return here because we want to continue to evaluate the other constraints\\n589: \\t\\t}\\n590: \\n591: \\t\\tif \\t\\t\\t\\t// can short circuit here since we had at least one match\\n598: \\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n599: \\t\\t\\tdefault:\\n600: \\t\\t\\t\\t// keep looping as we need to match all constraints\\n601: \\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n602: \\t\\t\\t}\\n603: \\t\\t} else {\\n604: \\t\\t\\t// no match\\n605: \\t\\t\\tswitch segmentMatchType {\\n606: \\t\\t\\tcase core.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:\\n607: \\t\\t\\t\\t// we can short circuit because we must match all constraints\\n608: \\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n609: \\t\\t\\tdefault:\\n610: \\t\\t\\t\\t// keep looping to see if we match the next constraint\\n611: \\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n612: \\t\\t\\t}\\n613: \\t\\t}\\n614: \\t}\\n615: \\n616: \\tmatched := true\\n617: \\n618: \\tswitch segmentMatchType {\\n619: \\tcase \\t\\t}\\n624: \\n625: \\tcase core.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:\\n626: \\t\\tif len(constraints) > 0 && constraintMatches == 0 {\\n627: \\t\\t\\treason = \\\"did not match ANY constraints\\\"\\n628: \\t\\t\\tmatched = false\\n629: \\t\\t}\\n630: \\tdefault:\\n631: \\t\\treason = fmt.Sprintf(\\\"unknown match type %d\\\", segmentMatchType)\\n632: \\t\\tmatched = false\\n633: \\t}\\n634: \\n635: \\treturn matched, reason, nil\\n636: }\\n637: \\n638: func crc32Num(entityID string, salt string) uint {\\n639: \\treturn uint(crc32.ChecksumIEEE([]byte(salt+entityID))) % totalBucketNum\\n640: }\\n641: \\n642: const (\\n643: \\t// totalBucketNum represents how many buckets we can use to determine the consistent hashing\\n644: \\t// distribution and rollout\\n645: \\ttotalBucketNum uint = \t\t\tresp.Match = false\n267: \t\t\treturn resp, nil\n268: \t\t}\n269: \n270: \t\td := validDistributions[index]\n271: \t\ts.logger.Debug(\"matched distribution\", rpcevaluation.EvaluationReason_MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON\n277: \t\treturn resp, nil\n278: \t} // end rule loop\n279: \n280: \treturn resp, nil\n281: }\n282: \n283: // Boolean evaluates a request for a have exhausted all rollouts and we still don't have a match, return flag enabled value.\n446: \tresp.Reason = rpcevaluation.EvaluationReason_DEFAULT_EVALUATION_REASON\n447: \tresp.Enabled =
